-m lerobot.scripts.lerobot_eval --policy.path=/root/autodl-tmp/models/smolvla_libero --env.type=libero --env.task=libero_object --eval.n_episodes=20 --eval.batch_size=1 --policy.device=cuda\", \"server\": \"bjb1-18228\", \"model_name\": \"smolvla_libero\", \"data_path\": \"libero_object\", \"data_count\": 20, \"seed\": 1000, \"checkpoint_path\": \"/root/autodl-tmp/models/smolvla_libero\", \"log_path\": \"/tmp/lerobot_eval_object2.log\"}, \"conclusion\": \"success\", \"completed_at\": \"2026-05-22T11:29:28.083Z\", \"notes\": \"## Root Cause Analysis (post-experiment)\\n\\nThe 66.5% vs 96% gap is explained by MODEL MISMATCH, not eval pipeline bugs:\\n\\n**Paper setup (96% on LIBERO-Object):**\\n- ONE unified multi-task model trained on ALL 40 LIBERO tasks (spatial+object+goal+long, 1693 episodes)\\n- 100,000 steps, batch_size=64\\n- Frozen vision encoder + action expert from scratch\\n\\n**Our local model (66.5% on LIBERO-Object):**\\n- `pepijn223/smolvla_libero` trained ONLY on `libero_spatial` (10 tasks)\\n- 25,000 steps, batch_size=32\\n- Same as official `lerobot/smolvla_libero` on HF Hub\\n\\n**Key findings:**\\n1. No publicly available checkpoint matches the paper's multi-task 100K-step model\\n2. GitHub issue #3287 confirms another researcher also got ~70% trying to reproduce\\n3. Official `lerobot/smolvla_libero` and `lerobot/smolvla_libero_plus` are both trained only on libero_spatial\\n4. The 66.5% result is expected for a spatial-only model evaluated on object tasks (out-of-distribution)\\n\\n**Implications for our wrapper (10.8%):**\\n- Wrapper still has a real bug (10.8% vs 66.5% = 6x gap with same model)\\n- But even with a perfect wrapper, we cannot reach 96% without the correct multi-task checkpoint\\n- Wrapper bugs likely: missing open-loop chunk replay, state dim mismatch, image preprocessing\"}, \"eval-pi05-vla-only-object-seed42-r4\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"retry_count\": 0, \"created_at\": \"2026-05-22T10:54:01.041Z\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-22T11:26:20.250Z\",
